14: ## 🛠️ Confirmed Tech Stack (Pure React / Next.js)
15: - **Framework**: Next.js 14+ (App Router, Pure JavaScript / JSX, React 18)
16: - **Styling & UI**: Tailwind CSS, CSS Variables, Lucide Icons, Modern Bento-Grid UI

19: - **Security & Sessions**: `bcryptjs` credential hashing (DOB format `YYYY-MM-DD` and Admin passphrase), `jose` encrypted JWTs in HTTP-only cookies (`exam_portal_session`), Next.js Edge Middleware route guards.

2: 
3: > [!IMPORTANT]
4: > **Phase 8 Implementation Status: 🟢 COMPLETED & VERIFIED**  
5: > All features are built with **Pure React + Next.js (JavaScript / JSX)** and Tailwind CSS. No TypeScript is used in the codebase. All examination results, verified marks, letter grades, and certified remarks are persistently stored in SQLite (`prisma/dev.db`).

11: ![Phase 8 Results & Marksheet Verification](file:///C:/Users/adhav/.gemini/antigravity-ide/brain/884387c4-bbff-4b94-a621-f26ee7ea4f09/phase8_results_verification_1793032392553.webp)

16: 
17: ## 🎯 What Was Built in Phase 8
18: 
19: ### 1. Database Schema & Models
20: Utilized the [`ExamResult`](file:///d:/DYNAMIC%20EXAM%20SITE/prisma/schema.prisma) model in SQLite:
21: - **`ExamResult`**:
22:   - Relates `ExamSchedule` and `StudentProfile` with a unique constraint `@@unique([examScheduleId, studentId])`.
23:   - Fields: `marksObtained` (Float), `maxMarks` (Float), `remarks` (String?), `isPublished` (Boolean), `createdAt`.
24: 
25: ### 2. Comprehensive Backend APIs
26: | Method | Route | Description | Security / Guard |
27: |---|---|---|---|
28: | `GET` | `/api/admin/results` | Lists scheduled exams, enrolled candidates, submissions, and marks | COE Admin role check |
29: | `POST` | `/api/admin/results` | Records or updates marks, validates ranges, computes grade, and saves to SQLite | COE Admin role check |
30: | `PATCH` | `/api/admin/results/[id]` | Instant Draft <-> Live toggle for published marksheet | COE Admin role check |
31: | `DELETE` | `/api/admin/results/[id]` | Removes a specific result record from SQLite | COE Admin role check |
32: | `GET` | `/api/student/results` | Fetches certified statement of marks with overall GPA and subject breakdown | Strict server-side class isolation |
33: 
34: ### 3. Controller of Examinations (COE) Marks Entry Suite ([`/coe-admin/results`](file:///d:/DYNAMIC%20EXAM%20SITE/src/app/coe-admin/results/page.jsx))
35: - **Standard Filter Controls**: filter candidates by **All Standards**, **Class 10 (Brother)**, or **Class 12 (Sister)**.
36: - **Candidate Submission Link**: displays live submission indicator, time spent (e.g. `1m 43s`), and auto-scored preliminary objective marks.
37: - **Autofill Objective Score**: 1-click button to pull the candidate's test hall score directly into the marks input.
38: - **Interactive Grading**:
39:   - Automatically calculates percentage and assigns letter grade (`A+`, `A`, `B+`, `B`, `C`, `D`, `E`).
40:   - Input field for custom COE remarks and subject feedback.
41: - **Independent Publishing Toggle**:
42:   - Results can be saved privately as **Drafts** while evaluations are pending.
43:   - One-click **Publish Result to Student** activates the result immediately on the student portal.
44: 
45: ### 4. Student Official Statement of Marks ([`/student/results`](file:///d:/DYNAMIC%20EXAM%20SITE/src/app/student/results/page.jsx))
46: - **Board Certification Header**:
47:   - Tamil Nadu Samacheer Kalvi emblem and COE Verified certification.
48:   - Candidate details: Name, Roll Number (`TN2026-0901`), School Grade (Class 9), Active Syllabus (Class 10 Prep), Medium, Academic Year.
49: - **Performance Overview Bento Metrics**:
50:   - Overall Percentage with Letter Grade badge (e.g. `95% • Grade A+ (Outstanding)`).
51:   - Total Evaluated Papers count.
52:   - Cumulative Marks scored vs maximum possible.
53:   - Top Performing Subject.
54: - **Subject-wise Evaluation Table**:
55:   - Subject Name, Code (`MAT-10`), Exam Date.
56:   - Maximum Marks, Marks Scored, Percentage Progress Bar.
57:   - Letter Grade and Academic Classification.
58:   - Official certified COE remarks.
59: - **Print Utility**: Print Marksheet button configured with print-friendly CSS.
60:

[1] \\r\\n- Eye-Friendly Dark Mode / Low-Glare Theme\\r\\n  - Characteristics: Charcoal or deep navy backgrounds (#121824 or #0F172A) paired with muted text and soft accent tones (teal, cool blue, or amber).\\r\\n  - Best For: Long-duration exams, late-night study sessions, and reducing eye strain. [1] \\r\\n- High-Contrast Accessibility (WCAG 2.1 AAA) Theme\\r\\n  - Characteristics: Pure black/white contrasts, thick focus rings, scaleable typography, and colorblind-safe status indicators (using shapes or patterns alongside red/green flags).\\r\\n  - Best For: Inclusive testing environments and institutional compliance. [1] \\r\\n- Gamified / Micro-Interaction Theme\\r\\n  - Characteristics: Subtle progress bars, streak counters, smooth timer transitions, and instant feedback card animations.\\r\\n  - Best For: Practice quizzes, mock tests, and K-12 or language-learning prep apps. [1, 2, 3, 4, 5] \\r\\n- Modular SaaS Dashboard Theme\\r\\n  - Characteristics: Clean bento-grid layouts, split-screen architecture (question panel on the left/center, question palette/navigator grid on the right), and pinned countdown timers.\\r\\n  - Best For: All-in-one exam portals, student dashboards, and proctored test environments. [1] \\r\\n- Eye-Friendly Dark Mode / Low-Glare Theme\\r\\n  - Characteristics: Charcoal or deep navy backgrounds (#121824 or #0F172A) paired with muted text and soft accent tones (teal, cool blue, or amber).\\r\\n  - Best For: Long-duration exams, late-night study sessions, and reducing eye strain. [1] \\r\\n- High-Contrast Accessibility (WCAG 2.1 AAA) Theme\\r\\n  - Characteristics: Pure black/white contrasts, thick focus rings, scaleable typography, and colorblind-safe status indicators (using shapes or patterns alongside red/green flags).\\r\\n  - Best For: Inclusive testing environments and institutional compliance. [1] \\r\\n- Gamified / Micro-Interaction Theme\\r\\n  - Characteristics: Subtle progress bars, streak counters, smooth timer transitions, and instant feedback card animations.\\r\\n  - Best For: Practice quizzes, mock tests, and K-12 or language-learning prep apps. 5. **`coe-mastercontrol.vercel.app`** &mdash; Highlights the administrative controller power.

7: function parseDateTime12h(dateStr, timeStr) {
8:   try {
9:     const baseDate = dateStr ? new Date(dateStr) : new Date();
10:     if (!timeStr) return baseDate;
11: 
12:     // Handle formats like "09:30 AM", "2:00 PM", "14:30"
13:     const match = timeStr.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*(AM|PM)?$/i);
14:     if (!match) return baseDate;
15: 
16:     let hours = parseInt(match[1], 10);
17:     const minutes = parseInt(match[2], 10);
18:     const ampm = match[3] ? match[3].toUpperCase() : null;
19: 
20:     if (ampm === "PM" && hours < 12) hours += 12;
21:     if (ampm === "AM" && hours === 12) hours = 0;
22: 
23:     baseDate.setHours(hours, minutes, 0, 0);
24:     return baseDate;
25:   } catch {
26:     return new Date();
27:   }
28: }
29:

53:     setLoadingSubjects(true);

55:       .then((r) => r.json())
56:       .then((data) => {

65:           setSelectedSubjects([]);

70: 
71:     return () => { ignore = true; };

75:     setSelectedSubjects((prev) =>
76:       prev.includes(code) ? prev.filter((c) => c !== code) : [...prev, code]
77:     );
78:   };
79: 
80:   if (done) {
81:     return (
82:       <div className="text-center py-8 px-2 space-y-5 animate-scale-in">
83:         <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">

98:     e.preventDefault();

101:       return;
102:     }

30: function parseFlexibleDuration(val) {
31:   if (typeof val === "number" && !isNaN(val)) return val;
32:   if (typeof val === "string") {
33:     const trimmed = val.trim();
34:     const hMatch = trimmed.match(/^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:h|hr|hrs|hours)$/i);
35:     if (hMatch) return Math.round(parseFloat(hMatch[1]) * 60);
36:     const mMatch = trimmed.match(/^(\d+)\s*(?:m|min|mins|minutes)?$/i);
37:     if (mMatch) return parseInt(mMatch[1], 10);
38:   }
39:   return 180;
40: }
41: 
42: export async function POST(request) {
43:   try {
44:     const session = await getCurrentSession();
45:     if (!session || session.role !== "ADMIN") {
46:       return NextResponse.json(
47:         { error: "Access denied. Administrator privileges required." },
48:         { status: 403 }
49:       );
50:     }
51: 
52:     const payload = await request.json();
53: 
54:     if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") {
55:       return NextResponse.json(
56:         { error: "Invalid JSON payload." },
57:         { status: 400 }
58:       );
59:     }

67:       }
68:     } catch { showNotice("Error updating exam status."); }

7: function parseDateTime12h(dateStr, timeStr) {
8:   try {
9:     const baseDate = dateStr ? new Date(dateStr) : new Date();
10:     if (!timeStr) return baseDate;
11: 
12:     // Handle formats like "09:30 AM", "2:00 PM", "14:30"
13:     const match = timeStr.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*(AM|PM)?$/i);
14:     if (!match) return baseDate;
15: 
16:     let hours = parseInt(match[1], 10);
17:     const minutes = parseInt(match[2], 10);
18:     const ampm = match[3] ? match[3].toUpperCase() : null;
19: 
20:     if (ampm === "PM" && hours < 12) hours += 12;
21:     if (ampm === "AM" && hours === 12) hours = 0;
22: 
23:     baseDate.setHours(hours, minutes, 0, 0);
24:     return baseDate;
25:   } catch {
26:     return new Date();
27:   }
28: }
29: 
30: function parseFlexibleDuration(val) {
31:   if (typeof val === "number" && !isNaN(val)) return val;
32:   if (typeof val === "string") {
33:     const trimmed = val.trim();
34:     const hMatch = trimmed.match(/^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:h|hr|hrs|hours)$/i);
35:     if (hMatch) return Math.round(parseFloat(hMatch[1]) * 60);
36:     const mMatch = trimmed.match(/^(\d+)\s*(?:m|min|mins|minutes)?$/i);
37:     if (mMatch) return parseInt(mMatch[1], 10);
38:   }
39:   return 180;
40: }

1: import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
2: import { PrismaClient } from "@prisma/client";
3: import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";
4: 
5: const prisma = new PrismaClient();
6: 
7: // Validation helpers
8: function sanitizeString(str) {
9:   if (typeof str !== "string") return "";
10:   return str.trim().replace(/[<>"'%;()&+]/g, "");
11: }
12: 
13: function isValidRollNo(rollNo) {
14:   // Format: TN followed by 4 digits, dash, 4 digits e.g. TN2026-0901
15:   return /^TN\d{4}-\d{4}$/.test(rollNo);
16: }
17: 
18: function isValidDate(dateStr) {
19:   if (!dateStr) return false;
20:   const d = new Date(dateStr);
21:   return !isNaN(d.getTime()) && dateStr.match(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/);
22: }
23: 
24: export async function POST(request) {
25:   try {
26:     const body = await request.json();
27: 
28:     // Extract and sanitize inputs
29:     const name = sanitizeString(body.name || "");
30:     const rollNo = sanitizeString(body.rollNo || "").toUpperCase();
31:     const dob = sanitizeString(body.dob || "");
32:     const language = body.language === "Tamil" ? "Tamil" : "English";
33: 
34:     // --- Input Validation ---
35:     if (!name || name.length < 2 || name.length > 100) {
36:       return NextResponse.json(
37:         { error: "Full name must be between 2 and 100 characters." },
38:         { status: 400 }
39:       );
40:     }
41: 
42:     if (!isValidRollNo(rollNo)) {
43:       return NextResponse.json(
44:         { error: "Invalid Roll Number format. Expected format: TN2026-0901" },
45:         { status: 400 }
46:       );
47:     }
48: 
49:     if (!isValidDate(dob)) {
50:       return NextResponse.json(
51:         { error: "Invalid date of birth. Please use YYYY-MM-DD format." },
52:         { status: 400 }
53:       );
54:     }
55: 
56:     // Age check: must be between 10 and 25 years
57:     const dobDate = new Date(dob);
58:     const today = new Date();
59:     const ageMs = today - dobDate;
60:     const ageYears = ageMs / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 365.25);
61:     if (ageYears < 10 || ageYears > 25) {
62:       return NextResponse.json(
63:         { error: "Date of birth does not meet age requirements (10–25 years)." },
64:         { status: 400 }
65:       );
66:     }
67: 
68:     // --- Check for duplicate Roll No ---
69:     const existing = await prisma.user.findUnique({
70:       where: { username: rollNo },
71:     });
72: 
73:     if (existing) {
74:       return NextResponse.json(
75:         { error: "This Roll Number is already registered. Please contact the COE if you believe this is an error." },
76:         { status: 409 }
77:       );
78:     }
79: 
80:     // Extract class assignments (Classes 1 to 12)
81:     const currentClass = Math.min(12, Math.max(1, parseInt(body.currentClass, 10) || 10));
82:     const examPrepClass = Math.min(12, Math.max(1, parseInt(body.examPrepClass, 10) || (currentClass === 9 ? 10 : currentClass === 11 ? 12 : currentClass)));
83:     const selectedSubjectCodes = Array.isArray(body.selectedSubjectCodes) ? body.selectedSubjectCodes : [];
84: 
85:     // --- Hash the DOB as password ---
86:     const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(dob, 12);
87: 
88:     // --- Create pending user and profile with requested subjects ---
89:     const newUser = await prisma.user.create({
90:       data: {
91:         username: rollNo,
92:         passwordHash,
93:         role: "PENDING", // Will be changed to "STUDENT" by COE upon approval
94:         profile: {
95:           create: {
96:             fullName: name,
97:             currentClass: currentClass,
98:             examPrepClass: examPrepClass,
99:             medium: language,
100:             board: "Tamil Nadu Samacheer Kalvi",

   ]
 };
 
+function parseDisplayDuration(val) {
+  if (typeof val === "number" && !isNaN(val)) return val;
+  if (typeof val === "string") {
+    const trimmed = val.trim();
+    const hMatch = trimmed.match(/^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:h|hr|hrs|hours)$/i);
+    if (hMatch) return Math.round(parseFloat(hMatch[1]) * 60);
+    const mMatch = trimmed.match(/^(\d+)\s*(?:m|min|mins|minutes)?$/i);
+    if (mMatch) return parseInt(mMatch[1], 10);
+  }
+  return 180;
+}

+                            {u.chapters?.length > 0 && (
+                              <span className="text-[10px] text-[#5c5c8a] ml-1">
+                                ({u.chapters.length} chapter{u.chapters.length > 1 ? "s" : ""})
+                              </span>
+                            )}
+                          </div>
+                        ))}
+                      </div>
+                    </div>
+                  )}
+
                   <div className="p-3 glass-sm rounded-xl space-y-1">
                     <span className="text-[10px] text-[#5c5c8a] block">Scheduled Conducting Time</span>
                     <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-xs text-[#f0f0ff]">
[diff_block_end]

### 1. NotebookLLM Exam Setter Master Prompt & JSON Format

Copy and paste this prompt directly into **NotebookLLM** whenever you upload a textbook chapter or past question paper. It generates the exact format the COE Exam Setter engine expects:

```text
Act as the official Tamil Nadu Samacheer Kalvi & DGE Controller of Examinations Question Paper Setter.
Based on the provided source materials, generate an examination question paper in strict JSON format matching this exact schema:

{
  "examTitle": "Class 10 Mathematics Public Practice Examination 2026",
  "subjectCode": "MAT-10",
  "subjectName": "Mathematics",
  "prepClass": 10,
  "medium": "English",
  "examDate": "2026-11-20",
  "startTime": "09:30 AM",
  "time": "180m",
  "totalMarks": 100,
  "passingMarks": 35,
  "instructions": "Dear candidate, please read each question carefully. Manage your 180 minutes steadily. Best wishes!",
  "status": "PUBLISHED",
  "paperType": "PUBLIC_EXAM",
  "publishSchedule": true,
  "publishSyllabus": true,
  "syllabusUnits": [
    {
      "unitNumber": 1,
      "title": "Relations and Functions",
      "chapters": [
        { "chapterNumber": 1, "title": "Ordered Pairs & Cartesian Product" },
        { "chapterNumber": 2, "title": "Relations & Types of Functions" }
      ]
    }
  ],
  "questions": [
    {
      "questionNumber": 1,
      "section": "Part I — 1 Mark Multiple Choice Questions",
      "marks": 1,
      "questionText": "If n(A × B) = 6 and A = {1, 3}, then n(B) is:",
      "options": [
        { "id": "A", "text": "1" },
        { "id": "B", "text": "2" },
        { "id": "C", "text": "3" },
        { "id": "D", "text": "6" }
      ],
      "correctAnswer": "C",
      "explanation": "Since n(A × B) = n(A) × n(B) and n(A) = 2, we have 6 = 2 × n(B) => n(B) = 3."
    }
  ]
}

Formatting Rules:
1. "time" or "durationMinutes" can be "180m", "3h", or 180 (for 3 hours).
2. "startTime" must be in 12-Hour AM/PM format (e.g., "09:30 AM").
3. Include step-by-step "explanation" for every question.
4. Output ONLY raw JSON without markdown backticks or preamble.
```

---

### 2. What Has Been Completed & Verified

#### A. COE Admin JSON Exam Setter ([`/coe-admin/exam-setter`](https://ak-coe-exam.vercel.app/coe-admin/exam-setter))
- **Flexible Duration Parsing**: Accepts `"180m"`, `"3h"`, `180`, or `"90m"`, cleanly displayed in the pre-flight check card as `180 mins (3 hrs)`.
- **Publishing Toggles**:
  - `[x] Publish Schedule to Student Portal immediately`: Auto-creates the exam entry in the active student calendar.
  - `[x] Import & Publish Syllabus Units & Chapters`: Persists syllabus units and chapters into the syllabus database.
- **Fast 1-Click Presets**:
  - Class 10 Math (180 mins / 3 hrs)
  - Class 12 Physics (180 mins / 3 hrs)
  - Class 12 Computer Science (180 mins / 3 hrs)

#### B. Practice Assessment Engine ([`/student/syllabus`](https://ak-coe-exam.vercel.app/student/syllabus))
- Inside the student syllabus explorer, students can switch between **"Syllabus"** and **"Start Practice"**.
- Self-study quizzes with question timers and choice navigation.
- **Instant Result Evaluation**: Immediate percentage score calculation and step-by-step worked explanations without affecting official COE board records.

#### C. Class 1 to 12 Range & Dynamic Subject Selection ([`/`](https://ak-coe-exam.vercel.app/))
- Registration supports **Classes 1 through 12** for both Current Class and Exam Prep Class.
- **Smart Stream Mapping**:
  - **Class 10**: Tamil, English, Mathematics, Science, Social Science.
  - **Class 12**: All official TN DGE subjects across Science, Arts, and Commerce (Tamil, English, Mathematics, Physics, Chemistry, Biology, Botany, Zoology, Computer Science, Commerce, Accountancy, Economics, Business Maths, etc.).
- Selected subjects link to the candidate's profile upon registration.

#### D. COE Student Verification Hub ([`/coe-admin/student-preview`](https://ak-coe-exam.vercel.app/coe-admin/student-preview))
- Real-time simulator allowing the COE administrator to select any student (e.g., Brother `TN2026-0901` or Sister `TN2026-1102`).
- Inspects their active exam notices, countdown timers, enrolled subjects, and official marksheets.

#### E. Navigation Bar Updates ([`Navbar.jsx`](file:///d:/DYNAMIC%20EXAM%20SITE/src/components/layout/Navbar.jsx))
- Dynamically adapts based on user role:
  - **COE Admin**: `COE Control`, `Exam Setter (JSON)`, `Student Preview`, `Students`, `Exams`, `Marks Entry`, `Syllabus`, `Papers`.
  - **Student**: `Dashboard`, `Syllabus & Practice`, `Question Papers`, `Marksheet`.

1: import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
2: import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";
3: import { db } from "@/lib/db";
4: import { signSession, getSessionCookieOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
5: import { checkRateLimit, resetRateLimit } from "@/lib/rateLimit";
6: 
7: export async function POST(request) {
8:   try {
9:     const body = await request.json();
10:     const { rollNo, dob } = body;
11: 
12:     if (!rollNo || !dob) {
13:       return NextResponse.json(
14:         { error: "Roll Number and Date of Birth are both required." },
15:         { status: 400 }
16:       );
17:     }
18: 
19:     const cleanRollNo = rollNo.trim().toUpperCase();
20:     const cleanDob = dob.trim(); // Format: YYYY-MM-DD
21: 
22:     // 1. Rate limiting check (max 5 failed attempts per roll number)
23:     const rateLimit = checkRateLimit(`student_${cleanRollNo}`);
24:     if (!rateLimit.allowed) {
25:       return NextResponse.json(
26:         {
27:           error: `Too many failed login attempts. Please wait ${rateLimit.retryAfterSeconds} seconds before trying again.`,
28:         },
29:         { status: 429 }
30:       );
31:     }
32: 
33:     // 2. Fetch user (any role — we'll check role after)
34:     const user = await db.user.findFirst({
35:       where: { username: cleanRollNo },
36:       include: { profile: true },
37:     });
38: 
39:     if (!user || !user.profile) {
40:       return NextResponse.json(
41:         { error: "Invalid Roll Number or credentials." },
42:         { status: 401 }
43:       );
44:     }
45: 
46:     // 2a. Block PENDING accounts with a clear message
47:     if (user.role === "PENDING") {
48:       return NextResponse.json(
49:         {
50:           error:
51:             "Your registration is pending COE approval. You will be notified once your account is activated.",
52:         },
53:         { status: 403 }
54:       );
55:     }
56: 
57:     // 2b. Ensure only STUDENT role can use this endpoint
58:     if (user.role !== "STUDENT") {
59:       return NextResponse.json(
60:         { error: "Invalid Roll Number or credentials." },
61:         { status: 401 }
62:       );
63:     }
64: 
65:     // 3. Compare DOB against bcrypt passwordHash
66:     const isMatch = await bcrypt.compare(cleanDob, user.passwordHash);
67:     if (!isMatch) {
68:       return NextResponse.json(
69:         {
70:           error: `Incorrect Date of Birth. ${rateLimit.remainingAttempts} attempts remaining.`,
71:         },
72:         { status: 401 }
73:       );
74:     }
75: 
76:     // 4. Reset rate limiter on successful match
77:     resetRateLimit(`student_${cleanRollNo}`);
78: 
79:     // 5. Sign session token
80:     const sessionPayload = {
81:       userId: user.id,
82:       username: user.username,
83:       role: "STUDENT",
84:       profileId: user.profile.id,
85:       fullName: user.profile.fullName,
86:       currentClass: user.profile.currentClass,
87:       examPrepClass: user.profile.examPrepClass,
88:       board: user.profile.board,
89:       medium: user.profile.medium,
90:     };
91:

94:     // 6. Return response with HTTP-only cookie

96:       success: true,
97:       message: `Welcome, ${user.profile.fullName}!`,
98:       user: sessionPayload,
99:     });
